[07:12:54 +24204.5s]       [vterms] READ: MOVED-OUT Δ ≈ 0 with MOVED-IN Δ carrying the gap ⇒ the sides agree on how much VAMP leaves and disagree on where it lands, i.e. vshare. [07:12:54 +24204.5s]       [terms] READ: MOVED-OUT Δ ≈ 0 and MOVED-IN Δ large ⇒ the sides agree on how much volume LEAVES and disagree on where it LANDS — look at POOL (moved_tot, the per-cell pool) vs pshare (the recipient slice, which includes the max-share water-fill each side implements in a DIFFERENT place).
